 INTEGER PROCEDURE SPLIT_MSTS(QUE_MSTS);                                0000111300380000  
 %+-------------------------------------------------------------------+ 0000111400381000  
 %                                                                      0000111500382000  
 % OPERACION:                                                           0000111600383000  
 % - SE LE PASA 1 PARAMETR.                                             0000111700384000  
 %   1.- ARRAY [0]. CONTIENE EL VALOR DEL MYSELF.TASKSTRING             0000111800385000  
 %                                                                      0000111900386000  
 % RESPUESTA:                                                           0000112000387000  
 % - REGRESA LOS VALORES:                                               0000112100388000  
 %   0  = NADA                                                          0000112200389000  
 %   -1 = SI EL TAM DEL PARAMETRO ES MUY PEQUENO                        0000112300390000  
 %   >0 = NUMERO DE CAMPOS SEPARADOS                                    0000112400391000  
 %                                                                      0000112500392000  
 ARRAY QUE_MSTS[*];                                                     0000112600393000  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000112700394000  
  INTEGER                                                               0000112800395000  
   I, QUE_POS,RSLT_SM,LON,TAM,RESTO;                                    0000112900396000  
  POINTER                                                               0000113000397000  
   P1;                                                                  0000113100398000  
  SKANNER_DECS;                                                         0000113200399000  
  ARRAY                                                                 0000113300400000  
   AFAULT[0:59];                                                        0000113400401000  
  POINTER                                                               0000113500402000  
   PAFAULT;                                                             0000113600403000  
  REAL                                                                  0000113700404000  
   RFAULT;                                                              0000113800405000  
  LABEL                                                                 0000113900406000  
   FIN_SPLIT_MSTS;                                                      0000114000407000  
  %                                                                     0000114100408000  
  EPILOG PROCEDURE E_SPLIT_MSTS;                                        0000114200409000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000114300410000  
   SPLIT_MSTS:= RSLT_SM;                                                0000114400411000  
   END OF E_OLSPLIT_MSTS;                                               0000114500412000  
  %                                                                     0000114600413000  
  PAFAULT:= P(AFAULT);                                                  0000114700414000  
  ON ANYFAULT [PAFAULT:RFAULT]:                                         0000114800415000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000114900416000  
   OL_DISPLAY(1,"ERR>",TITSW CAT ": ",                                  0000115000417000  
              "Procedure failed: 'Split_MSTS', " CAT                    0000115100418000  
              "# " CAT ST(RFAULT,*) CAT                                 0000115200419000  
              " @ " CAT ST(PAFAULT,80));                                0000115300420000  
   GO FIN_SPLIT_MSTS;                                                   0000115400421000  
   END;                                                                 0000115500422000  
  %                                                                     0000115600423000  
  RSLT_SM:= 0;                                                          0000115700424000  
  LON:= SIZE(QUE_MSTS) * 6;                                             0000115800425000  
  P1:= P(QUE_MSTS);                                                     0000115900426000  
  SCAN P1:P1 FOR RESTO:LON UNTIL EQL NULO;                              0000116000427000  
  TAM:= LON - RESTO;                                                    0000116100428000  
  IF TAM GTR 5 THEN                                                     0000116200429000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000116300430000  
   QUE_POS:= 0;                                                         0000116400431000  
   FOR I:= 1 STEP 1 UNTIL MAX_MSTS DO                                   0000116500432000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000116600433000  
    IF MSTSINFO_FIELD_NAME(I) IN VALID_CHARS FOR 1 THEN                 0000116700434000  
     BEGIN                                                              0000116800435000  
     PT    := P(QUE_MSTS)+QUE_POS;                                      0000116900436000  
     TOKEN := SCR;                                                      0000117000437000  
     C:= (SIZE(QUE_MSTS) * 6) - QUE_POS;                                0000117100438000  
     SKANIT;                                                            0000117200439000  
     IF TOKEN = MSTSINFO_FIELD_NAME(I) FOR LEN AND                      0000117300440000  
        LEN   = MSTSINFO_FIELD_SIZE(I) THEN                             0000117400441000  
      BEGIN                                                             0000117500442000  
      DO SKANIT UNTIL TEXT_IS("=") OR TOKE = ENDV;                      0000117600443000  
      IF TEXT_IS("=") THEN                                              0000117700444000  
       BEGIN                                                            0000117800445000  
       SKANIT;                                                          0000117900446000  
       IF TOKE NEQ ENDV THEN                                            0000118000447000  
        BEGIN                                                           0000118100448000  
        MSTSINFO_FIELD_OK(I):= 1;                                       0000118200449000  
        MSTSINFO_VAL_SIZE(I):= LEN;                                     0000118300450000  
        REP MSTSINFO_VAL_DATA(I) BY TOKEN FOR LEN;                      0000118400451000  
        RSLT_SM:= * + 1;                                                0000118500452000  
        END;                                                            0000118600453000  
       END;                                                             0000118700454000  
      END;                                                              0000118800455000  
     DO SKANIT UNTIL TOKE = SEMICOLONV OR TOKE = ENDV;                  0000118900456000  
     QUE_POS:= OFFSET(PT);                                              0000119000457000  
     END;                                                               0000119100458000  
    END; % FOR                                                          0000119200459000  
   END                                                                  0000119300460000  
  ELSE                                                                  0000119400461000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000119500462000  
   RSLT_SM:= -1;                                                        0000119600463000  
   END;                                                                 0000119700464000  
  %                                                                     0000119800465000  
  FIN_SPLIT_MSTS:                                                       0000119900466000  
  END DE SPLIT_MSTS;                                                    0000120000467000  
 %                                                                      0000120100468000  
 %+-------------------------------------------------------------------+ 0000120200469000  
 PROCEDURE CARGADEF;                                                    0000120300470000  
 %+-------------------------------------------------------------------+ 0000120400471000  
 %                                                                      0000120500472000  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000120600473000  
  DEFINE                                                                0000120700474000  
   MR_DEF        = 14 #,                                                0000120800475000  
   BS_DEF        = 420#;                                                0000120900476000  
  ARRAY                                                                 0000121000477000  
   REG_DEF[0:MR_DEF-1],                                                 0000121100478000  
   T_DEF[0:19],                                                         0000121200479000  
   F_DEF[0:9];                                                          0000121300480000  
  EBCDIC ARRAY                                                          0000121400480200  
   SCR_HHMM[0:99];                                                      0000121500480400  
  INTEGER                                                               0000121600481000  
   LR_DEF,I, LON,RESTO, TAM;                                            0000121700482000  
  BOOLEAN                                                               0000121800483000  
   RSLT_IO_DEF;                                                         0000121900484000  
  REAL                                                                  0000122000485000  
   RSLT_OC_DEF;                                                         0000122100486000  
  POINTER                                                               0000122200487000  
   P1;                                                                  0000122300488000  
  SKANNER_DECS;                                                         0000122400489000  
  FILE                                                                  0000122500490000  
   DEF (KIND        = DISK,                                             0000122600491000  
        MAXRECSIZE  = MR_DEF,                                           0000122700492000  
        BLOCKSIZE   = BS_DEF,                                           0000122800493000  
        NEWFILE     = FALSE);                                           0000122900494000  
  %                                                                     0000123000495000  
  %+------------------------------------------------------------------+ 0000123100496000  
  EPILOG PROCEDURE E_CARGADEF;                                          0000123200497000  
  %+------------------------------------------------------------------+ 0000123300498000  
  %                                                                     0000123400499000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000123500500000  
   IF P(ARY_DELAY_PRO) IN END_CHARS FOR 1 OR                            0000123600501000  
      (NOT (P(ARY_DELAY_PRO) IN NUMBERS FOR 2)) THEN                    0000123700502000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000123800503000  
    RNULO(ARY_DELAY_PRO);                                               0000123900504000  
    REP ARY_DELAY_PRO BY DEF_DELAY_PRO;                                 0000124000505000  
    END;                                                                0000124100506000  
   IF P(ARY_SNDTOSPLK) IN END_CHARS FOR 1 THEN                          0000124200507000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000124300508000  
    RNULO(ARY_SNDTOSPLK);                                               0000124400509000  
    REP ARY_SNDTOSPLK BY DEF_SNDTOSPLK;                                 0000124500510000  
    END;                                                                0000124600511000  
   IF P(ARY_GRABA_LOG) IN END_CHARS FOR 1 THEN                          0000124700512000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000124800513000  
    RNULO(ARY_GRABA_LOG);                                               0000124900514000  
    REP ARY_GRABA_LOG BY DEF_GRABA_LOG;                                 0000125000515000  
    END;                                                                0000125100516000  
   IF P(ARY_FWDR_NAME) IN END_CHARS FOR 1 THEN                          0000125200517000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000125300518000  
    RNULO(ARY_FWDR_NAME);                                               0000125400519000  
    REP ARY_FWDR_NAME BY DEF_FWDR_NAME;                                 0000125500520000  
    END;                                                                0000125600521000  
   IF P(ARY_S151L001CTL) IN END_CHARS FOR 1 THEN                        0000125700522000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000125800523000  
    RNULO(ARY_S151L001CTL);                                             0000125900524000  
    REP ARY_S151L001CTL BY DEF_S151L001CTL;                             0000126000525000  
    END;                                                                0000126100526000  
   IF P(ARY_DIR_FAM) IN END_CHARS FOR 1 THEN                            0000126200527000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000126300528000  
    RNULO(ARY_DIR_FAM);                                                 0000126400529000  
    REP ARY_DIR_FAM BY DEF_DIR_FAM;                                     0000126500530000  
    END;                                                                0000126600531000  
   IF P(ARY_LOG_FAM) IN END_CHARS FOR 1 THEN                            0000126700532000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000126800533000  
    RNULO(ARY_LOG_FAM);                                                 0000126900534000  
    REP ARY_LOG_FAM BY DEF_LOG_FAM;                                     0000127000535000  
    END;                                                                0000127100536000  
   IF P(ARY_HHMM_CORTE) IN END_CHARS FOR 1 OR                           0000127200537000  
      (NOT (P(ARY_HHMM_CORTE) IN NUMBERS FOR 4)) THEN                   0000127300538000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000127400539000  
    RNULO(ARY_HHMM_CORTE);                                              0000127500540000  
    REP ARY_HHMM_CORTE BY DEF_HHMM_CORTE;                               0000127600541000  
    END;                                                                0000127700542000  
   IF P(ARY_HHMM_PRO_INI) IN END_CHARS FOR 1 OR                         0000127800543000  
      (NOT (P(ARY_HHMM_PRO_INI) IN NUMBERS FOR 4)) THEN                 0000127900544000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000128000545000  
    RNULO(ARY_HHMM_PRO_INI);                                            0000128100546000  
    REP ARY_HHMM_PRO_INI BY DEF_HHMM_PRO_INI;                           0000128200547000  
    END;                                                                0000128300548000  
   IF P(ARY_HHMM_PRO_FIN) IN END_CHARS FOR 1 OR                         0000128400549000  
      (NOT (P(ARY_HHMM_PRO_FIN) IN NUMBERS FOR 4)) THEN                 0000128500550000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000128600551000  
    RNULO(ARY_HHMM_PRO_FIN);                                            0000128700552000  
    REP ARY_HHMM_PRO_FIN BY DEF_HHMM_PRO_FIN;                           0000128800553000  
    END;                                                                0000128900554000  
   %                                                                    0000129000555000  
   DELAY_PRO:= INTEGER(P(ARY_DELAY_PRO),2);                             0000129100556000  
   IF P(ARY_SNDTOSPLK) EQL "S" OR P(ARY_SNDTOSPLK) EQL "SI" THEN        0000129200557000  
    SNDTOSPLK:= TRUE                                                    0000129300558000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000129400559000  
    SNDTOSPLK:= FALSE;                                                  0000129500560000  
   IF P(ARY_GRABA_LOG) EQL "S" OR P(ARY_GRABA_LOG) EQL "SI" THEN        0000129600561000  
    GRABA_LOG:= TRUE                                                    0000129700562000  
   ELSE                                                                 0000129800563000  
    GRABA_LOG:= FALSE;                                                  0000129900564000  
   HHMM_CORTE:= INTEGER(P(ARY_HHMM_CORTE),4);                           0000130000565000  
   IF HHMM_CORTE LSS 0001 OR                                            0000130100566000  
      HHMM_CORTE GTR 1500 THEN                                          0000130200567000  
    HHMM_CORTE:= DECIMAL(DEF_HHMM_CORTE);                               0000130300568000  
   HHMM_CORTE:= HHMM_CORTE * 100;                                       0000130400569000  
   REP SCR_HHMM BY HHMM_CORTE FOR 6 DIGITS;                             0000130500569500  
   HORA_INI:= HHMM_CORTE;                                               0000130600570000  
   HORA_FIN:= HHMM_CORTE + 030000;                                      0000130700571000  
   HHMM_PRO_INI:= INTEGER(P(ARY_HHMM_PRO_INI),4);                       0000130800572000  
   IF HHMM_PRO_INI LSS 0001 OR                                          0000130900573000  
      HHMM_PRO_INI GTR 1500 THEN                                        0000131000574000  
    HHMM_PRO_INI:= DECIMAL(DEF_HHMM_PRO_INI);                           0000131100575000  
   HHMM_PRO_INI:= HHMM_PRO_INI * 100;                                   0000131200576000  
   HHMM_PRO_FIN:= INTEGER(P(ARY_HHMM_PRO_FIN),4);                       0000131300577000  
   IF HHMM_PRO_FIN LSS 1500 OR                                          0000131400578000  
      HHMM_PRO_FIN GTR 2359 THEN                                        0000131500579000  
    HHMM_PRO_FIN:= DECIMAL(DEF_HHMM_PRO_FIN);                           0000131600580000  
   HHMM_PRO_FIN:= HHMM_PRO_FIN * 100;                                   0000131700581000  
   HHMM_PRO_FIN:= HHMM_PRO_FIN + 001000;                                0000131800582000  
   %                                                                    0000131900583000  
   OL_DISPLAY(1,"INF>",TITSW CAT ": ",                                  0000132000584000  
              "PARAMETROS CARGADOS:");                                  0000132100585000  
   OL_DISPLAY(1,"INF>",TITSW CAT ": ",                                  0000132200586000  
       "FWDR_NAME    : " CAT ST(P(ARY_FWDR_NAME),DEF_FWDR_NAME_LEN));   0000132300587000  
   OL_DISPLAY(1,"INF>",TITSW CAT ": ",                                  0000132400588000  
       "HHMM_CORTE   : " CAT ST(SCR_HHMM,6) CAT  " (HHMMSS)");          0000132500589000  
   OL_DISPLAY(1,"INF>",TITSW CAT ": ",                                  0000132600590000  
       "DELAY_PRO    : " CAT ST(DELAY_PRO,2));                          0000132700591000  
   OL_DISPLAY(1,"INF>",TITSW CAT ": ",                                  0000132800592000

 PROCEDURE CARGA_B01SISDIA;                                             0000204701323000  
 %+-------------------------------------------------------------------+ 0000204801324000  
 %                                                                      0000204901325000  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000205001326000  
  EBCDIC ARRAY                                                          0000205101327000  
   EARY_CSD[0:999];                                                     0000205201328000  
  INTEGER                                                               0000205301329000  
   RSLT_CSD,                                                            0000205401330000  
   I1;                                                                  0000205501331000  
  LABEL                                                                 0000205601332000  
   FIN_CARGA_B01SISDIA;                                                 0000205701333000  
  ARRAY                                                                 0000205801334000  
   AFAULT[0:59];                                                        0000205901335000  
  POINTER                                                               0000206001336000  
   PAFAULT;                                                             0000206101337000  
  REAL                                                                  0000206201338000  
   RFAULT;                                                              0000206301339000  
  %                                                                     0000206401340000  
  EPILOG PROCEDURE EPI_CARGA_B01SISDIA;                                 0000206501341000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000206601342000  
   END DE EPI_CARGA_B01SISDIA;                                          0000206701343000  
  %                                                                     0000206801344000  
  PAFAULT:= P(AFAULT);                                                  0000206901345000  
  ON ANYFAULT [PAFAULT:RFAULT]:                                         0000207001346000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000207101347000  
   OL_DISPLAY(1,"ERR>",TITSW CAT ":",                                   0000207201348000  
           "FALLA EN PROC.: CARGA_B01SISDIA, " CAT                      0000207301349000  
           "# " CAT ST(RFAULT,*) CAT                                    0000207401350000  
           " @ " CAT ST(PAFAULT,80));                                   0000207501351000  
   GO FIN_CARGA_B01SISDIA;                                              0000207601352000  
   END;                                                                 0000207701353000  
  %                                                                     0000207801354000  
                                                                        0000207901355000  
  % SE LIMPIA LA TABLAS                                                 0000208001356000  
  FOR I1:= 0 STEP 1 UNTIL MAX_SIS DO                                    0000208101357000  
   REP P(TAB_SIS[I1,0]) BY NULO FOR TAB_SIS_LEN WORDS;                  0000208201358000  
  FOR I1:= 1 STEP 1 UNTIL MAX_CSD DO                                    0000208301359000  
   REP P(TAB_CSD[I1,0]) BY NULO FOR TAB_CSD_LEN WORDS;                  0000208401360000  
                                                                        0000208501361000  
  %                                                                     0000208601362000  
  FOR I1:=1 STEP 1 UNTIL MAX_CSD DO                                     0000208701363000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000208801364000  
   RNULOE(EARY_CSD);                                                    0000208901365000  
   REP P(TAB_CSD[I1,0]) BY NULO FOR TAB_CSD_LEN WORDS;                  0000209001366000  
   REP EARY_CSD BY                                                      0000209101367000  
    "01",                     % FUNCION 1 - CONS S151B01SISDIA          0000209201368000  
    VARY_SIS[I1] FOR 4 DIGITS,% SISTEMA                                 0000209301369000  
    DATOS_HOST[0] FOR 2;      % CSI                                     0000209401370000  
   RSLT_CSD:=CONSISDIA(EARY_CSD);                                       0000209501371000  
   IF RSLT_CSD EQL 0 THEN                                               0000209601372000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000209701373000

 PROCEDURE CARGA_B09CONXSIS;                                            0000213401410000

  % SE LIMPIA LA TABLAS                                                 0000216601442000  
  FOR I1:= 0 STEP 1 UNTIL MAX_CXS DO                                    0000216701443000  
   REP P(TAB_CXS[I1,0]) BY NULO FOR TAB_CXS_LEN WORDS;                  0000216801444000  
                                                                        0000216901445000  
  %                                                                     0000217001446000  
  FOR I1:=1 STEP 1 UNTIL SIS_TOT DO                                     0000217101447000  
   BEGIN                                                                0000217201448000  
   FOR I2:=1 STEP 1 UNTIL GLO_NUM_CON DO                                0000217301449000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000217401450000  
    CXS_TOT:= CXS_TOT + 1;                                              0000217501451000

     SIS_CXS_INX_INI(I1):= CXS_TOT;                                     0000217801454000

      "01",                         % FUNCION 1 - CONS S151B09CONXSIS   0000218401460000

     BEGIN                                                              0000219101467000

     CXS_CTL_STA(CXS_TOT):= 1;                                          0000219601472000  
     CXS_CTL_FUN(CXS_TOT):= 1;                                          0000219701473000  
     CXS_CTL_SIS(CXS_TOT):= CSD_CTL_SIS(I1);                            0000219801474000  
     CXS_CTL_REN(CXS_TOT):= I2;                                         0000219901475000

     BEGIN                                                              0000220301479000

     CXS_CTL_STA(CXS_TOT):= 0;                                          0000220801484000  
     CXS_CTL_FUN(CXS_TOT):= 1;                                          0000220901485000  
     CXS_CTL_SIS(CXS_TOT):= CSD_CTL_SIS(I1);                            0000221001486000  
     CXS_CTL_REN(CXS_TOT):= I2;                                         0000221101487000  
     REP CXS_REG(CXS_TOT) BY EARY_CXS[0] FOR CXS_REG_LEN;               0000221201488000  
     REP CXS_NOMREN(CXS_TOT) BY " " FOR CXS_NOMREN_LEN;                 0000221301489000

   END; % FOR I1                                                        0000221601492000

   "TOTAL DE CONCEPTOS CARGADOS: ",ST(CXS_TOT,*);                       0000221901495000  
  ESCRIBE_LOG(LR_LOG,"INF",ARY_MSG);                                    0000222001496000  
  %FOR I1:=1 STEP 1 UNTIL 2 DO                                          0000222101497000  
  % BEGIN                                                               0000222201498000  
  % DISPLAY("I1:" CAT ST(I1,*));                                        0000222301499000

      END;                                                              0000368802902000

      (DT_SCR.[17:06] EQL 15 OR                % MINUTO 15              0000385603070000  
       DT_SCR.[17:06] EQL 30 OR                % MINUTO 30              0000385703071000
